        var KotertCount = 0
        var CardCount = 0
        var LestKotert = ""

        for (i = 0; i < Json.length; i++) {
            if (Json[i].Kotert) {
                $("#Labels").append(`<div style="font-size: 20px; font-weight: bold; display: flex; align-items: center; grid-gap: 15px; padding-right: 9px; margin: 7px 0 5px 0;padding-top: 8px;${(KotertCount == 0 ? "" : "border-top: 1px solid #d1d1d1;")}">${Json[i].Kotert}</div>`)
                KotertCount++
                LestKotert = Json[i].Kotert
                continue;
            }

            (function (i) {
                $("#Labels").append(`<div class="BtScroll" id="BtScroll_${Json[i].ID}" style="background: rgb(244 246 247); padding: 3px 9px; font-weight: 600; color: rgb(34, 34, 34); cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 6px;">${Json[i].Name} </div>`)

                $("#BtScroll_" + Json[i].ID).click(function () {
                    ScrollUp('#Card_' + Json[i].ID, 80)
                    $('.BtScroll').css('background', 'rgb(244 246 247)').css('color', '#222222')
                    $('#BtScroll_' + Json[i].ID).css('background', '#1d6294').css('color', 'white')
                })

                if (CardCount != 0 && i != Json.length) { $("#AllData").append(`<div style="max-width: 100%; height: 2px; background: #c1c1c1; margin: 50px;"></div>`) }
                $("#AllData").append(`<div id="Card_${Json[i].ID}" style="width: 100%;font-size: 18px;"><div style="font-size: 40px; font-weight: bold; display: flex; align-items: center; grid-gap: 15px;"><div style='display: flex ; grid-gap: 10px; align-items: flex-end;'><div style='display: inline-flex ; align-items: center; grid-gap: 11px;'>${(LestKotert == "× ××¨×× ×§××¨×" ? `<img style="width: 32px; margin-top: 4px;" src="https://www.matara.pro/nedarimplus/Mechubad/Reports/Files/Wait_NedarimCard.png">` : `<img style="width: 36px; margin-top: 4px;" src="https://matara.pro/nedarimplus/logo.png">`)}${Json[i].Name}</div><span style="font-size: 19px; color: #3a3a3a; display: inline-block; padding: 0px 4px 5.5px 0;"> ${LestKotert} </span></div><div class="Bt" id="CardBtCopy_${Json[i].ID}" style="font-size: 17px; cursor: pointer; height: 40px; width: 40px; display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; border-radius: 6px; color: #1d6294;gap: 5px;" ><i class="fa-solid fa-link"></i></div></div>${Json[i].Html}</div>`)


                $("#CardBtCopy_" + Json[i].ID).click(function () {
                    CopyText('https://matara.pro/nedarimplus/ApiDocumentation.html?v=' + Json[i].ID)

                    $("#CardBtCopy_" + Json[i].ID).html('<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check"></i> ×××¢×ª×§ ××××').css('color', '#009688').css('width', '120px')

                        $("#CardBtCopy_" + Json[i].ID).html('<i class="fa-solid fa-link"></i>').css('color', '#1d6294').css('width', '40px')
                    }, 2000)
                })
            })(i);

            CardCount++
        }


        if (GetURLParameter("V") !== "" && GetURLParameter("V") !== "undefined" && GetURLParameter("V") !== undefined) {
            $('.BtScroll').css('background', 'rgb(244 246 247)').css('color', '#222222')
            $('#BtScroll_' + GetURLParameter("V")).css('background', '#1d6294').css('color', 'white')

            setTimeout(function () { ScrollUp("#Card_" + GetURLParameter("V"), 80) }, 300)
        }


        var ScrollTimeout
        $(window).scroll(function () {
            clearTimeout(ScrollTimeout)

            ScrollTimeout = setTimeout(function () {
                var ScrollWindow = $(window).scrollTop();
                for (i = 0; i < Json.length; i++) {
                    if (Json[i].Kotert) { continue; }

                    if (ScrollWindow < (($("#Card_" + Json[i].ID).offset().top + $('#Card_' + Json[i].ID).outerHeight()) - 150)) {
                        window.history.replaceState(null, null, "?v=" + Json[i].ID);
                        break;
                    }
                }


                $('.BtScroll').css('background', 'rgb(244 246 247)').css('color', '#222222')
                $('#BtScroll_' + Json[i].ID).css('background', '#1d6294').css('color', 'white')


                var ScrollParent = $("#Labels").scrollTop()
                var OffsetChild = $('#BtScroll_' + Json[i].ID).offset().top - $('#Labels').offset().top

                $("#Labels").scrollTop(0)
                $("#Labels").scrollTop($('#BtScroll_' + Json[i].ID).offset().top - ($('#Labels').offset().top + 150))


            }, 50)
        });



        function ScrollUp(Input, Top) {
            if ($(Input).html() == undefined) return false;
            if (Top == undefined) Top = 200
            if ($(Input).attr('type') == undefined) {
                $('html, body').animate({ scrollTop: $(Input).position().top - Top }, 150);
            }
            else {
                $('html, body').animate({ scrollTop: $(Input).parent().position().top - Top }, 150);
            }
        }


        function GetURLParameter(sParam) {
            var sPageURL = window.location.search.substring(1);
            var sURLVariables = sPageURL.split('&');
            for (var i = 0; i < sURLVariables.length; i++) {
                var sParameterName = sURLVariables[i].split('=');
                if (sParameterName[0].toLowerCase() == sParam.toLowerCase()) {
                    return sParameterName[1];
                }
            }
            return ''
        }


        function CopyText(Text, Id) {

            $('#inputCopyText').val(Text).select();
            document.execCommand("copy");
        }



        function getUnusedIDs(maxID = 100) {
            let usedIDs = [];
            Json.forEach(item => {
                if (item.ID) {
                    usedIDs.push(item.ID);
                }
            });
            let allIDs = Array.from({ length: maxID }, (_, i) => (i + 1).toString());
            let unusedIDs = allIDs.filter(id => !usedIDs.includes(id));
            return unusedIDs;
        }

    </script>
